     THE BOARD                                                                 董事會
     The Board oversees the management of the business and affairs of          董 事 會 監 督 本 公 司 業 務 及 事 務 的 管 理。董 事 負
     the Company. The Directors are accountable for making decisions           責作出客觀決定以符合股東整體最佳利益。
     objectively in the best interest of the shareholders as a whole.

     the Company’s affairs, including the approval and monitoring of key       包括批准及監察主要政策事宜、整體策略、業務

     internal control and risk management systems, material transactions,      大交易、重大資本支出、委任董事及其他重大財
     major capital expenditure, appointment of Directors and other             務及營運事宜。
     significant financial and operational matters.


     The Board may delegate aspects of its management and                      董 事 會 可 向 管 理 層 轉 授 其 管 理 及 行 政 職 能。尤
     administration functions to the management. In particular, the            其本公司的日常管理已轉授予本集團的行政總

     team.

     Selection Criteria                                                         甄選標準

            Group is engaged in;


     (3)    commitment in respect of available time and relevant interest;      (3)   可投入的時間及對相關利益的承擔；

            professional experience, skills and length of service;                    經驗、技能及服務任期；


     (5)    qualifications which include professional qualifications, skills,   (5)   資歷，包括與本公司業務及企業策略相關
            knowledge and experience that are relevant to the Company’s               的專業資格、技能、知識及經驗；
            business and corporate strategy;


     (6)    the number of existing directorships and other commitments          (6)   現有董事人數以及其他可能需要候選人關
            that may demand the attention of the candidate;                           注的承擔；


     (7)    requirement for the Board to have Independent Non-executive         (7)   董事會根據上市規則有關委任獨立非執行
            Directors in accordance with the Listing Rules and whether the            董事的規定，以及候選人參照上市規則第
            candidates would be considered independent with reference to              3.13條所載的獨立指引是否被視為獨立；
            the independence guidelines set out in Rules 3.13 of the Listing
            Rules;


     (8)    Board Diversity Policy of the Company and any measurable            (8)   本公司的董事會成員多元化政策以及董事
            objectives adopted by the Nomination Committee for achieving              會為實現董事會成員多元化而採納的任何

Director Nomination Procedure                                              董事提名程序
Subject to the provisions in the Articles of Association of the            根 據 本 公 司 組 織 章 程 細 則 及 上 市 規 則 的 規 定，
Company and the Listing Rules, if the Board recognises the need            如董事會認為有需要新增一名董事或一名高級
for an additional Director or a member of senior management, the           管理人員，則會遵循下列程序：
following procedure will be followed:


(1)   The Nomination Committee and/or Board will identify potential        (1)   提名委員會及╱或董事會將根據甄選標準
      candidates based on the criteria as set out in the selection               所載標準確定潛在候選人，並可能需要外
      criteria, possibly with assistance from external agencies and/or           部機構及╱或顧問的協助；
      advisors;


(2)   The Nomination Committee and/or the Company Secretary of             (2)   本公司提名委員會及╱或公司秘書隨後將
      the Company will then provide the Board with the biographical              向董事會提供候選人的履歷詳情、候選人
      details and details of the relationship between the candidate              與本公司及╱或董事之間關係詳情、所擔
      and the Company and/or Directors, directorships held, skills               任 董 事 職 務、技 能 與 經 驗、需 要 大 量 時 間
      and experience, other positions which involve significant time             投入的其他職位以及上市規則、開曼群島
      commitment and any other particulars required by the Listing               公司法及其他監管規定就董事會任命候選
      Rules, the Companies Law of the Cayman Islands and other                   人要求的其他詳情；
      regulatory requirements for any candidate for appointment to
      the Board;


(3)   The Nomination Committee would then make recommendation              (3)   提名委員會隨後將建議候選人以及任職的
      to the Board on the proposed candidate(s) and the terms and                條款及條件向董事會提出建議；
      conditions of the appointment;


(4)   The Nomination Committee should ensure that the proposed             (4)   提名委員會應確保建議候選人將增強董事
      candidate(s) will enhance the diversity of the Board, being                會成員的多元化，尤其需關注性別平衡；
      particularly mindful of gender balance;


(5)   In the case of the appointment of an Independent                     (5)   如任命一名獨立非執行董事，提名委員會
      Non-executive Director, the Nomination Committee and/                      及╱或董事會應獲取與擬任董事有關的所
      or the Board should obtain all information in relation to the              有 資 料，使 董 事 會 可 根 據 上 市 規 則 第3.13
      proposed Director to allow the Board to adequately assess                  條（ 經 聯 交 所 可 能 不 時 作 出 修 訂）所 載 因
      the independence of the Director in accordance with the                    素充分評估董事的獨立性；及
      factors set out in Rules 3.13 of the Listing Rules, subject to any
      amendments as may be made by the Stock Exchange from
      time to time; and


(6)   The Board will then deliberate and decide on the appointment         (6)   董事會隨後將根據提名委員會之推薦建議
      based upon the recommendation of the Nomination                            審議及決定任命。
      Committee.

     sets out the approach to achieve a sustainable and balanced               而政策載列本公司取得持續平衡發展及本公司

     on the Board.

     relating to its own business model and specific needs from time to        董 事 會 帶 來 的 價 值 及 所 作 的 貢 獻。本 公 司 將 以
     time. The ultimate decision is based on merit and contribution that       客觀條件考慮人選並充分顧及董事會成員多元

The Company has adopted a dividend policy (“Dividend Policy”),            本 公 司 已 採 納 股 息 政 策（「股 息 政 策」），據 此，
pursuant to which the Company may declare and distribute                  本公司可向本公司股東（「股東」）宣派及派發股
dividends to the shareholders of the Company (the “Shareholders”)         息，使股東能分享本公司之溢利成果，同時使本
to allow Shareholders to share the Company’s profits and for the          公司能為未來增長保留充足儲備。
Company to retain adequate reserves for future growth.

     scheme (the “Share Option Scheme”) for the purpose of recognising      （「購 股 權 計 劃」），以 肯 定 及 嘉 許 曾 經 或 可 能 對
     and acknowledging the contributions the eligible participants had      本 集 團 作 出 貢 獻 的 合 資 格 參 與 人 士。根 據 購 股

     are quantitatively significant to the consolidated financial        management;

     of the reporting period.                                            December 2023 to the underlying financial records,

獨立核數師報告（續）


Key Audit Matter                                                 How our audit addressed the Key Audit Matter
關鍵審核事項                                                           吾等的審核如何處理該事項


Allowance for expected credit loss of the trade receivables (Continued)
貿易應收款項之預期信貸虧損撥備（續）


For the purpose of performing the assessment of the ECL,
the Directors of the Company engaged an independent
professional valuer to perform a valuation regarding ECL
on trade receivables. The determination of ECL required
significant management judgement to determine the key
assumptions including (i) expected credit loss rates for
each category of trade receivables; (ii) aging and overdue
analysis of the trade receivables; (iii) information regarding
the ability and intent of the customers to pay and
historical data on default rates; and (iv) shared credit risk
characteristics of trade receivables groups and market
conditions. The Group calibrates the matrix to adjust the
historical credit loss experience with forward-looking
information.
就評估預期信貸虧損而言， 貴公司董事委聘獨立專業
估值師根據董事的假設對應收貿易款項的預期信貸虧損
進行估值。釐定預期信貸虧損需要作出重大管理層判斷
以確定主要假設，包括(i)各類貿易應收款項預期信貸虧
損率；(ii)貿易應收款項的賬齡及逾期結餘分析；(iii)有關
客戶支付能力及意圖的資料以及違約率的過往記錄；及(iv)
貿易應收款項組別的共同信貸風險特點及市況。 貴集
團會校正矩陣以按前瞻性資料調整過往信貸虧損經驗。

2.   APPLICATION OF NEW AND AMENDMENTS                                 2.    新訂及經修訂國際財務報告準則
     TO INTERNATIONAL FINANCIAL                                             （「國際財務報告準則」）之應用
     REPORTING STANDARDS (“IFRSs”) (Continued)                              （續）
     New and amendments to IFRSs that are                                   於本年度強制生效之新訂及經
     mandatorily effective for the current year                             修訂國際財務報告準則（續）
     (Continued)
     2.1   Impacts on application of Amendments to IAS 8                    2.1   應用國際會計準則第8號之修訂會計
           Definition of Accounting Estimates                                     估計的定義的影響
           The Group has applied the amendments for the first time                本集團於本年度首次應用該等修訂。
           in the current year. The amendments define accounting                  該 等 修 訂 定 義 會 計 估 計 為「存 在 計
           estimates as “monetary amounts in financial statements                 量不明朗因素之財務報表之貨幣金
           that are subject to measurement uncertainty”. An                       額」。會計政策可能規定對涉及計量
           accounting policy may require items in financial                       不明朗因素之財務報表之項目進行
           statements to be measured in a way that involves                       計量。於此情況下，一間實體應編製
           measurement uncertainty. In such a case, an entity                     會 計 估 計，旨 在 達 到 會 計 政 策 載 列
           develops an accounting estimate to achieve the objective               之目標。國際會計準則第8號之修訂
           set out by the accounting policy. The amendments to IAS                闡明會計估計變更與會計政策變更
           8 clarify the distinction between changes in accounting                及錯誤更正之間的區別。
           estimates, and changes in accounting policies and the
           correction of errors.


           The application of the amendments in the current year                  於本年度應用該等修訂不會對綜合
           had no material impact on the consolidated financial                   財務報表產生重大影響。
           statements.


     2.2   Impacts on application of Amendments to IAS                      2.2   應用國際會計準則第1號之修訂及國
           1 and IFRS Practice Statement 2 Disclosure of                          際財務報告準則實務報告第2號會計
           Accounting Policies                                                    政策的披露的影響
           The Group has applied the amendments for the first                     本集團於本年度首次應用該等修訂。
           time in the current year. IAS 1 Presentation of Financial              國際會計準則第1號財務報表之呈列

           information is material if, when considered together                   資 料 一 併 考 慮 時，會 計 政 策 資 料 可

           financial statements make on the basis of those financial

         asset or liability at the measurement date. Fair value                  量及╱或披露用途之公平值乃按

         similarities to fair value but are not fair value, such as net          際會計準則第2號存貨 中之可變現

                                                                               的稅率計算。

         corresponding tax bases used in the computation                       遞延稅項負債一般就所有應課稅
         of taxable profit. Deferred tax liabilities are generally             暫時差額確認入賬。遞延稅項資產
         recognised for all taxable temporary differences. Deferred            一般僅於可能有應課稅溢利用於
         tax assets are generally recognised for all deductible                對銷可扣稅暫時差額時就所有可
         temporary differences to the extent that it is probable               扣稅暫時差額確認入賬。
         that taxable profits will be available against which those
         deductible temporary differences can be utilised.

         temporary differences associated with investments in                  投資所產生應課稅暫時差額進行
         subsidiaries, except where the Group is able to control               確認，惟倘本集團能控制暫時差額
         the reversal of the temporary difference and it is                    之撥回，且暫時差額不可能於可見
         probable that the temporary difference will not reverse               將來撥回之情況則除外。因有關該
         in the foreseeable future. Deferred tax assets arising                等投資的可扣減暫時差額而產生
         from deductible temporary differences associated with                 的遞延稅項資產僅在可能產生足
         such investments and interests are only recognised to                 夠應課稅溢利以使用暫時差額利

         taxable profits against which to utilise the benefits of the          確認。

                   Property, plant and equipment                                           物業、廠房及設備

                   and equipment are stated in the consolidated statement                  按成本減其後累計折舊及累計減
                   of financial position at cost less subsequent accumulated               值虧損（如有）於綜合財務狀況表
                   depreciation and subsequent accumulated impairment                      列賬。
                   losses, if any.


                   When the Group makes payments for ownership interests                   當本集團就包括租賃土地及建築
                   of properties which includes both leasehold land and                    元素在內的物業的所有權權益作
                   building elements, the entire consideration is allocated                出付款時，全部代價於初始確認時
                   between the leasehold land and the building elements in                 按相對公平值的比例分配至租賃

         when a group entity becomes a party to the contractual                    訂約方，則金融資產及金融負債予

         delivery of assets within the time frame established by                   訂時限內交付資產之金融資產購
         regulation or convention in the market place.                             入或出售。


         Financial assets and financial liabilities are initially                  金融資產及金融負債初步以公平
         measured at fair value except for trade receivables                       值計量，惟源自客戶合約的應收賬

         measured in accordance with IFRS 15. Transaction costs                    準則第15號計量。直接歸屬於金融
         that are directly attributable to the acquisition or issue                資產和金融負債（除按公平值計入

         as appropriate, on initial recognition. Transaction costs                 接歸屬於收購按公平值計入損益

         or financial liabilities at FVTPL are recognised immediately              本立即於損益確認。
         in profit or loss.


         The effective interest method is a method of calculating                  實際利率法為計算於相關期間金
         the amortised cost of a financial asset or financial liability            融資產及金融負債攤銷成本及分
         and of allocating interest income and interest expense                    配利息收入和利息開支之方法。實
         over the relevant period. The effective interest rate is                  際利率為將於金融資產及金融負
         the rate that exactly discounts estimated future cash                     債預期使用期或較短年期（倘適用）
         receipts and payments (including all fees and points paid                 之估計未來現金收入及付款（包括
         or received that form an integral part of the effective                   構成實際利率、交易成本及其他溢
         interest rate, transaction costs and other premiums or                    價或折讓整體之已付或收取所有
         discounts) through the expected life of the financial                     費用和利率差價）準確貼現至初始
         asset or financial liability, or, where appropriate, a shorter            確認時之賬面淨值。
         period, to the net carrying amount on initial recognition.

     which the debtors operate at the end of the reporting period.           計的變化可能對評估結果產生重大影響，

                    Some of the Group’s financial instruments are measured               出 於 財 務 報 告 的 目 的，本 集 團 的 部

                    the fair value, the Group uses market-observable data to             公 平 值 時，本 集 團 在 可 獲 得 的 範 圍
                    the extent it is available. For instruments with significant         內 使 用 市 場 可 觀 察 數 據。對 於 具 有
                    unobservable inputs under Level 3, the Group engages                 第三級下重大不可觀察輸入值的工
                    third party qualified valuers to perform the valuation. The          具，本 集 團 聘 請 第 三 方 合 資 格 估 值

                    and inputs to the model.                                             值技術和模型的輸入數據。

                                                                                                   價）計量的公平值

                                  fail to meet Level 1, and not using significant                  到第一級的可觀察輸入
                                  unobservable inputs. Unobservable inputs are                     數 據 ）且 並 未 使 用 重 大

      44. EVENT AFTER THE REPORTING PERIOD                                  44. 報告期後事項
             On 28 December 2023, the Company approved the share              於二零二三年十二月二十八日，本公司於
             consolidation at the extraordinary general meeting, which        股東特別大會批准股份合併，該股份合併
             is on the basis that every 10 existing ordinary shares in        的基準為將本公司已發行及未發行股本中
             the issued and unissued share capital of the Company be          的每10股現有普通股合併為1股合併股份。
             consolidated into 1 consolidated share. With effect from 2       自二零二四年一月二日起，本公司已發行
             January 2024, the existing ordinary shares in the issued and     及未發行股本中的2,000,000,000股現有普
             unissued share capital of the Company of 2,000,000,000 have      通 股 已 合 併 為200,000,000股 合 併 股 份。上
             been consolidated into 200,000,000 consolidated shares.          述股份合併的詳情載於本公司日期為二零
             Details of the above share consolidation are set out in the      二三年十二月八日及二零二三年十二月
